display: inline-block;"><div class="gsap_split_letter gsap_split_letter20" aria-hidden="true" style="position: relative; display: inline-block; opacity: 0; visibility: hidden;">t</div><div class="gsap_split_letter gsap_split_letter21" aria-hidden="true" style="position: relative; display: inline-block; opacity: 0; visibility: hidden;">o</div></div> <div class="gsap_split_word gsap_split_word5" aria-hidden="true" style="position: relative; display: inline-block;"><div class="gsap_split_letter gsap_split_letter22" aria-hidden="true" style="position: relative; display: inline-block; opacity: 0; visibility: hidden;">h</div><div class="gsap_split_letter gsap_split_letter23" aria-hidden="true" style="position: relative; display: inline-block; opacity: 0; visibility: hidden;">e</div><div class="gsap_split_letter gsap_split_letter24" aria-hidden="true" style="position: relative; display: inline-block; opacity: 0; visibility: hidden;">l</div><div class="gsap_split_letter gsap_split_letter25" aria-hidden="true" style="position: relative; display: inline-block; opacity: 0; visibility: hidden;">p</div></div></h2></div><div class="faq-list"><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">Proof it works?</div><div id="w-node-aab6b6ac-a6b5-287e-29da-13e777302521-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">Multiple independent and internal trials — across crops, geographies, and seasons — show consistent yield and biomass gains compared to conventional programs. Results hold across wheat, sunflower, maize, vegetables, and more. Full trial data is available on <a href="https://www.farmminerals.com/science/croptab">our Science page</a>.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">Is CropTab™ fully approved for use?</div><div id="w-node-_1ac6146a-b13f-8d53-6742-476c5bfd7ab7-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">Yes. CropTab™ is fully registered under US fertilizer regulations, compliant with Regulation (EU) 2019/1009, and CE-certified for EU markets. Every batch is tested against strict contaminant limits before release.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">How can a 13 g tablet replace a 150 kg program?</div><div id="w-node-_10f0df96-2d74-2bc2-7eba-fb7ea8612660-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">Because the crop needs less to achieve the same yield. Nearly all of the applied nutrients are absorbed, and the tablet stimulates the plant to use soil and atmospheric supplies more efficiently. That means the plant eats less from outside to produce the same harvest.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">What about soil fixation and losses?</div><div id="w-node-bc16d6fb-1f39-701e-56d2-c05f66c2c689-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">The foliar pathway bypasses fixation entirely. In-furrow, the carbon capsule slows nutrient tie-up, giving the plant more time to take it in — reducing the losses that conventional fertilizers can’t avoid.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">Is CropTab™ just a biostimulant, or does it feed crops directly?</div><div id="w-node-e3837279-47ac-54e7-0cb8-dc832cf4c521-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">Both. Each tablet delivers measurable N, P, or K, but it also enhances the crop’s ability to draw on what’s already in the soil and air. That’s why the crop “needs to eat less” from fertilizer to reach the same yield.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">What’s in a tablet — balanced NPK or single nutrients?</div><div id="w-node-_57ca2ab0-c9ff-9f1f-32e3-c8be0ffa4fee-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">Each tablet contains one primary nutrient — N, P, or K — encapsulated for maximum absorption. This lets you target exactly what the crop needs, at the right stage, without overloading the system.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">Rate per hectare?</div><div id="w-node-be34bea7-bc34-19dc-f972-273b9e87b47d-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">1 tablet per 2.5 acres (1 hectare), per application. See the label for stage-specific timing.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">How do I mix it?</div><div id="w-node-e4664e13-1e3d-8c9c-ef9b-31f6e082fbcb-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">Drop the tablet into 5–10 L of clean water. Wait about 10 minutes for full dispersion, stir lightly, then top up to your spray volume.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">Does this replace my whole program?</div><div id="w-node-bd3e5144-b70a-c7f4-2c6d-75219794655d-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">It can, but most farmers start by swapping one or two passes. The rest of the program is adjusted once your own field data confirms performance.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">Does CropTab™ reduce the total nutrient requirement for my crop?<br></div><div id="w-node-e6bdb648-1791-b314-8ebd-dac4622ff439-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">Yes. Independent trials show that the same yield can be reached with far less external nutrient input. Plants become more efficient: they absorb the applied dose nearly completely, and they unlock soil and atmospheric reserves that would otherwise remain unused.<br></div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">Will grain protein or quality drop if I add less?</div><div id="w-node-_55458ee0-dce3-0e18-7eed-c1806ad94eb7-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">No. In wheat trials (Wildfarmed, 2025), protein levels (grain N) were maintained while phosphorus, and potassium in grain increased. This shows yields and quality can be sustained, even as external nutrient input is reduced.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">Tank-mix compatibility?</div><div id="w-node-_48fad657-656b-0883-cae5-e4db76299bd0-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">Works well with most herbicides, fungicides, biologicals, and other nutrients. A jar test is still the best way to confirm before large-scale mixing.</div></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab"><div class="sust-tab-heading"><div class="text-16-regular-caps green">Storage &amp; shelf life?</div><div id="w-node-_384397ac-7561-d220-cfc5-2478e6a40c52-f2c32eaa" class="batch-plus"><div class="plus-horizontal"></div><div class="plus-vertical" style="translate: none; rotate: none; scale: none; transform: translate(0px, 0px);"></div></div></div><div class="faq-tab-content" style="height: 0em;"><div class="faq-tab-content__text"><div class="text-16-regular green">Cool, dry, sealed storage is best. The tablets are stable for multiple seasons if kept out of direct sunlight and moisture.</div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><div class="footer-hero-wrap"></div></div></main>
